export function trainForecastBaseline({
    modelContract,
} = {}) {
    const contractValidation =
        assertModelContract(
            modelContract
        );

    if (
        !contractValidation.ok
    ) {
        return unavailableResult({
            modelContract,
            reason:
                contractValidation.reason,
        });
    }

    const materialization =
        modelContract.featureMaterialization;

    const rows =
        completeRows(
            materialization
        );

    if (
        !rows.length
    ) {
        return unavailableResult({
            modelContract,
            reason:
                FORECAST_BASELINE_REASON.INSUFFICIENT_COMPLETE_ROWS,
            completeRowCount:
                0,
        });

export function materializeForecastFeatures({
    featureSet,
} = {}) {
    const validatedFeatureSet =
        assertFeatureSet(
            featureSet
        );

    const rows =
        validRows(
            validatedFeatureSet.inputQualification
        );

    const matrix =
        rows.map(
            (
                row,
                index
            ) => {
                const features =
                    Object.fromEntries(
                        validatedFeatureSet.features.map(
                            (
                                feature
                            ) =>
                                [
                                    feature.name,
                                    materializeFeatureCell({
                                        rows,
                                        index,
                                        feature,
                                    }),
                                ]
                        )
                    );

export function buildFinancialBiKpiByKeyQuery({
    persistenceKey,
} = {}) {
    const normalizedPersistenceKey =
        requireNonEmptyString(
            persistenceKey,
            "PERSISTENCE_KEY"
        );

    if (
        normalizedPersistenceKey.length !==
            64 ||
        !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(
            normalizedPersistenceKey
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "PERSISTENCE_KEY_INVALID"
        );
    }

    return Object.freeze({
        phase:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_CONTRACT_PHASE,

        layer:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_CONTRACT_LAYER,

        upstreamPhase:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_CONTRACT_UPSTREAM_PHASE,

        endpoint:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_ENDPOINT.GET_KPI_BY_PERSISTENCE_KEY,

        method:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_HTTP_METHOD.GET,

FILE: C:\Users\owner\ASHMAAN\backend\services\financialBi\financialBiApiContract.js
LINE: 613
        persistenceKey:
            normalizedPersistenceKey.toLowerCase(),

        readOnly:
            true,

        boundaries:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_CONTRACT_BOUNDARIES,
    });
}


export function buildFinancialBiApiEnvelope({
    status,
    data = null,
    meta = null,
    error = null,
} = {}) {
    assertControlledValue({
        value:
            status,

        container:
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_RESPONSE_STATUS,

        field:
            "RESPONSE_STATUS",
    });

    if (
        status ===
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_RESPONSE_STATUS.OK &&
        error !==
            null
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "OK_RESPONSE_CANNOT_CONTAIN_ERROR"
        );
    }

    if (
        status !==
            FINANCIAL_BI_API_RESPONSE_STATUS.OK &&
        data !==
            null
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "NON_OK_RESPONSE_CANNOT_CONTAIN_DATA"

        createdAt:
            record.createdAt ??
            null,

        updatedAt:
            record.updatedAt ??
            null,
    });
}


export async function listFinancialBiKpis({
    query = {},
    repository,
} = {}) {
    validateRepository(
        repository
    );

    const apiQuery =
        buildFinancialBiKpiListQuery(
            query
        );

    const result =
        await repository
            .listKpis({
                filters:
                    apiQuery.filters,

                pagination:
                    apiQuery.pagination,

                sort:
                    apiQuery.sort,
            });

    if (
        !result ||
        !Array.isArray(
            result.items
        ) ||
        !Number.isInteger(
            result.total
        ) ||
        result.total <
            0

                sort:
                    apiQuery.sort,

                filters:
                    apiQuery.filters,
            }),
    });
}

export function executeFinancialBiKpiAggregation({ definition } = {}) {
    if (!definition || definition.phase !== FINANCIAL_BI_KPI_AGGREGATION_CONTRACT_PHASE) {
        throw new Error("FINANCIAL_BI_KPI_AGGREGATION_CONTRACT_REQUIRED");
    }

    const values = [];
    const diagnostics = [];
    let partial = false;

    for (const member of definition.members) {
        const calculation = member.calculation;

        if (!calculation || calculation.phase !== FINANCIAL_BI_CALCULATION_EXECUTION_PHASE) {
            if (member.required) {
                return unavailable(
                    definition,
                    FINANCIAL_BI_KPI_AGGREGATION_EXECUTION_REASON.INVALID_MEMBER_PHASE,
                    diagnostics
                );
            }

            partial = true;
            diagnostics.push(Object.freeze({
                name: member.name,
                used: false,
                reason: FINANCIAL_BI_KPI_AGGREGATION_EXECUTION_REASON.INVALID_MEMBER_PHASE,
            }));
            continue;
        }

        const usable =
            calculation.status === FINANCIAL_BI_CALCULATION_EXECUTION_STATUS.CALCULATED ||
            calculation.status === FINANCIAL_BI_CALCULATION_EXECUTION_STATUS.PARTIALLY_CALCULATED;

        const value = calculation.result?.value;

}

export async function persistFinancialBiKpi({ contract, repository } = {}) {
  if (!contract || contract.phase !== FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_CONTRACT_PHASE) {
    throw new Error("PHASE_10_7_PERSISTENCE_CONTRACT_REQUIRED");
  }

  if (typeof contract.persistenceKey !== "string" || contract.persistenceKey.length !== 64) {
    throw new Error("PERSISTENCE_KEY_INVALID");
  }

  validateRepository(repository);

  if (contract.payload.available !== true) {
    return Object.freeze({
      phase: FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_PHASE,
      layer: FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_LAYER,
      upstreamPhase: FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_UPSTREAM_PHASE,
      contract,
      status: FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.UNAVAILABLE,
      reason: FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_REASON.SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE,
      persistenceKey: contract.persistenceKey,
      document: null,
      persistence: Object.freeze({
        executed: false,
        inserted: false,
        existing: false,
        documentId: null,
      }),
      boundaries: FINANCIAL_BI_PERSISTENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_BOUNDARIES,
    });
  }

  const document = buildDocument(contract);
  const result = await repository.upsertByPersistenceKey({
    persistenceKey: contract.persistenceKey,
    document,
  });

  if (
    typeof document.toObject ===
      "function"
  ) {
    return document.toObject();
  }

  return document;

export function createMongooseOptimizationDssPersistenceRepository({
  model =
    OptimizationDssRecommendation,
} = {}) {
  if (
    !model ||
    typeof model.findOneAndUpdate !==
      "function"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "MONGOOSE_MODEL_REQUIRED"
    );
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    async upsertByPersistenceKey({
      persistenceKey,
      record,
    }) {
      // persistenceKey is the deterministic selector and must not be
      // written through both $set and $setOnInsert in the same update.
      // MongoDB rejects overlapping update paths such as:
      //   $set.persistenceKey + $setOnInsert.persistenceKey

      // updated idempotently.

          _recordPersistenceKey,

        record ?? {};

          {

      });

  return mongoFilter;
}

function normalizeTimestamp(value, field) {
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    throw new Error(`${field}_INVALID`);
  }
  return date.toISOString();
}

export function buildOptimizationDssHorizon({
  start,
  end,
  timezone,
} = {}) {
  const normalizedStart = normalizeTimestamp(start, "HORIZON_START");
  const normalizedEnd = normalizeTimestamp(end, "HORIZON_END");

  if (new Date(normalizedEnd) <= new Date(normalizedStart)) {
    throw new Error("HORIZON_RANGE_INVALID");
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    start: normalizedStart,
    end: normalizedEnd,
    timezone: requireNonEmptyString(timezone, "HORIZON_TIMEZONE"),
    semantics: OPTIMIZATION_DSS_TIME_SEMANTICS,
  });
}

export function buildOptimizationDssConstraint({
  name,
  kind,
  required = true,
  parameters = {},
} = {}) {
  requireControlled(
    kind,
    OPTIMIZATION_DSS_CONSTRAINT_KIND,
    "CONSTRAINT_KIND"
  );

  if (!parameters || typeof parameters !== "object" || Array.isArray(parameters)) {
    throw new Error("CONSTRAINT_PARAMETERS_INVALID");
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    name: requireNonEmptyString(name, "CONSTRAINT_NAME"),
    kind,
    required: Boolean(required),
    parameters: Object.freeze({ ...parameters }),
    evaluated: false,
    satisfied: null,
  });
}

export function qualifyOptimizationDssResult({
  executionResult,
} = {}) {
  if (
    !executionResult ||
    executionResult.phase !==
      OPTIMIZATION_DSS_SOLVER_EXECUTION_PHASE ||
    executionResult.layer !==
      OPTIMIZATION_DSS_SOLVER_EXECUTION_LAYER
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      OPTIMIZATION_DSS_RESULT_QUALIFICATION_REASON.EXECUTION_INVALID
    );
  }

  const solverResult =
    executionResult.solverResult;

  const builtModel =
    executionResult.builtModel ??
    executionResult
      .solverContract
      ?.builtModel ??
    null;

  if (
    !solverResult ||
    !builtModel
  ) {
    return Object.freeze({
      phase:
        OPTIMIZATION_DSS_RESULT_QUALIFICATION_PHASE,

      layer:
        OPTIMIZATION_DSS_RESULT_QUALIFICATION_LAYER,

}

  optimalityTolerance = 1e-6,

      "SOLVER_TOLERANCE_NEGATIVE"
    );
  }

  requirePositiveInteger(
    timeLimitSeconds,
    "TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS"
  );

  requirePositiveInteger(
    iterationLimit,
    "ITERATION_LIMIT"
